# Agents · D · part 28

947 entries, docs-reconciler to docs-writer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **docs-reconciler**: Resolves code/documentation contradictions surfaced by the auditor. MUST BE USED on /audit:fix when the finding type is "doc-drift". Updates documentation (comments, README, docstrings, OpenAPI specs) to match verified code behavior. Never modifies production code. ([bradselph/zero-trust-audit-team](https://github.com/bradselph/zero-trust-audit-team/blob/HEAD/agents/docs-reconciler.md))
- **docs-reconciler**: Record coverage dispositions, evidence pins and parity dimensions through update_docs_ledger; writes no file directly. ([drmoisan/drm-copilot](https://github.com/drmoisan/drm-copilot/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/docs-reconciler.md) · 4 copies)
- **docs-reconciler**: Keeps a repository's guidance documents honest against the code — ADR index vs files and statuses, the agent guide's workspace and service map vs the real projects, commands and stack, technical-debt registers vs fixed items, feature folders vs their indexes, living specs vs what shipped, and mirrored skill/agent copies in sync. Fixes the words, never the code. Use after a review, before a release, monthly, or when a doc "looks stale". ([m0x6A/claude-skills](https://github.com/m0x6A/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/review-kit/agents/docs-reconciler.md))
- **docs-reconciler**: Reconciles one documentation area (specs, plans, bug reports and test designs, skills and agents, instructions) against the current code and the documentation rulebook — fixes mechanical drift and volatile facts, flags judgment calls. Dispatched per area by the docs-reconciler skill. ([OleksandrOmelchenko/stackfoundry](https://github.com/OleksandrOmelchenko/stackfoundry/blob/HEAD/plugins/stackfoundry/agents/docs-reconciler.md) · 2 copies)
- **docs-reconstructor**: Two modes. PER-PHASE (every phase): an independent, read-only docs-drift audit — given the phase's diff, does any current-truth doc (README.md, docs/, architecture/, ai-docs/) now misdescribe the system? Scoped to what changed; verdict feeds the DoD gate. MILESTONE (Phase 64 only): full blank-slate reconstruction of the docs that ought to exist, from authoritative project reality, as a shadow proposal. Never overwrites docs/. Never edits — findings go back to docs-maintainer. ([ctosullivan/codecompass](https://github.com/ctosullivan/codecompass/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/docs-reconstructor.md))
- **docs-reference**: Specialized agent for looking up Protheus documentation - native functions, SX data dictionary, REST APIs, MV parameters, and TOTVS framework reference with local + TDN online search ([thalysjuvenal/advpl-specialist](https://github.com/thalysjuvenal/advpl-specialist/blob/HEAD/agents/docs-reference.md) · ★196 · 2 copies)

- **docs-refresher**: Targeted documentation refresh sub-agent. Reads the current branch's diff against a base ref, identifies docs/*.md files affected by changed code, rewrites only the stale sections in those docs, and commits the result as a separate "docs: refresh" commit on the branch. Returns a metrics block with counts (files changed, lines changed, skip reason if any). ([app-vitals/shipwright](https://github.com/app-vitals/shipwright/blob/HEAD/plugins/shipwright/agents/docs-refresher.md) · ★16)
